## ② 场景与人物档案

**场景**（observed, frame）：酒店客房。画面右侧为床（白色被褥），床头板深色；左侧为浅色木饰面墙板+深色衣柜门；天花板有嵌入式筒灯；暖色室内灯光。地面米色。

**人物 1（主体）**（observed）：女孩，约 8-10 岁儿童体型。
- 发型：黑色长发编两条麻花辫垂胸前，辫尾系白色/淡紫色缎带；两侧别粉色星形发卡（≥3 枚）；眼下有星形亮片贴纸（最后一帧清晰可见）。
- 服装：浅蓝紫（periwinkle）吊带背心，胸前卡通印花；内搭黄色肩带露出；下装亮粉色工装裤，腰部有荷叶边裙式层叠。
- 饰品：右手腕粉色串珠手链（多圈）；颈部彩色串珠项链。

**画外/背景行动者**（observed, frame@0.2-9.4）：床上躺着一名成年人，盖白被，全程持手机（疑似拍摄者/旁观者），仅露出头部与手臂，无大动作。不构成第二个舞蹈主体。

**道具清单**：床+被褥、衣柜、天花板灯、（人物佩戴）发卡/缎带/亮片贴纸/手链/项链。

## ③ 全程时间轴（因果动作链 + 机位分镜）

| 时间码 | 主体动作（因果链） | 机位/构图 |
|---|---|---|
| 0.00-0.5 | 起始姿态：右臂侧伸，头微右倾，面向镜头微笑 → 手臂收回启动摆动 | 中景，人物居中偏左 |
| 0.5-1.5 | 头左右大幅摆动（辫子甩动），双臂前后交替摆；嘴部开合（对镜头唱/跟唱口型，uncertain 是否真唱） | 同上，微晃 |
| 1.5-2.9 | 躯干左右摇摆，手臂大幅前后甩动，辫子随动量横扫；2.4s 处张嘴幅度大（重拍口型） | 同上 |
| 2.9-3.6 | 双手抬至脸侧/耳旁做俏皮手势（手指弯曲），身体前倾 | 人物稍近镜头 |
| 3.6-4.6 | 身体右转带动辫子甩出，右臂上举，随后回落；头部跟随转动 | 同上 |
| 4.6-5.8 | 右臂屈肘举至头侧（爪式手型），随节拍小跳/颠步，肩部上下 | 同上 |
| 5.8-7.5 | 双手在头部两侧做交替"猫爪"波浪/挥动，胯部左右摆；6.9s 张嘴笑 | 同上 |
| 7.5-9.0 | 大幅甩头转身（辫子横扫模糊），右臂上举划弧，身体回正 | 运动模糊帧 |
| 9.0-9.47 | 向镜头凑近，右手对镜头做手枪指/点指手势，咧嘴大笑收尾（终态 settled：定格于指镜头笑容） | 人物贴近镜头 |

**末三分之一倒查**：最后清楚可见状态=指镜头大笑；其后无黑场/字幕/转场；动作在指镜头处自然收势。文件终点=9.47s，最后一帧 9.43s 已核。

## ④ BPM 实测（onset_probe.py，sr=22050）

- ref_tempo（autocorr）= 63.8 BPM（参考值，非定论）
- 强 onset 链：1.23H 1.94H 2.64S | 4.75S 5.20H 5.69S 6.14S 6.63S 7.08H 7.57S 8.02S 8.50H 8.95H
- 5.20→8.95s 区间强音头等距 0.45-0.49s（均值≈0.469s）→ **主候选 ≈128 BPM（中置信）**；63.8 为半速候选（低置信）
- 2.64→4.75s 存在 2.1s 稀疏段（乐句气口/前奏）
- 判定：**固定节拍**（等距 kick 网格成立），可在提示词中以 "≈128 BPM" 作参考；精确对拍仍以时间码锚点为准
- 音画对账：5.2-9.0s 的高密度 onset 与人物的蹦跳/挥臂重拍大致同步（视觉重拍间隔≈0.45-0.5s，吻合）

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：实拍（AI 高置信=否，判定=实拍，高置信）**
- 证据 1：1.0s/3.4s/7.5s 运动模糊形态为真实卷帘快门特征（辫子拖影连续）。
- 证据 2：床上成年人持手机、被褥褶皱等背景细节全程稳定，无时序漂移。
- 证据 3：9.43s 原分辨率帧可见星形亮片贴纸、串珠手链的逐珠结构——AIGC 易崩细节完整。
- 反证检查：未见渐变形、肢体融合、手指异常、背景物体重排。

## ⑦ 完整提示词（T2VA，可直接粘贴）

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action, vertical 9:16 smartphone footage, a medium shot frames a cheerful Chinese girl around 8-10 years old dancing in a warmly lit hotel guest room. She has two long braided pigtails tied with pale ribbons, pink star hairclips, small star-shaped sequin gems under her eyes, a periwinkle camisole with a cartoon chest print and a visible yellow inner strap, loose bright-pink cargo pants with a ruffled skirt-layer at the waist, a pink beaded bracelet on her right wrist and a colorful bead necklace. Behind her on the right lies a white hotel bed where an adult lies under the duvet holding a phone, staying still throughout; pale wood wall panels and a dark wardrobe door fill the left background. The camera is handheld with slight breathing sway, framed from her thighs up. From 0.0 to 1.5 seconds she sways her head left and right in large arcs so the braids swing, sweeping her arms back and forth while mouthing along to the music. From 1.5 to 2.9 seconds her torso rocks side to side with wide arm swings, the braids whipping across the frame on the momentum. At 2.9 seconds she pulls both hands up beside her ears into playful curled-finger poses and leans toward the lens, then from 3.6 to 4.6 seconds twists her body right, sending one braid flying, and raises her right arm overhead before bringing it back down. From 4.6 to 5.8 seconds she holds a bent-elbow cat-paw hand beside her head while bouncing on the beat with small hops. From 5.8 to 7.5 seconds both hands alternate wavy cat-paw waves at head height as her hips sway left and right. At 7.5 seconds she whips her head through a fast turn, one braid sweeping across in a motion-blurred arc, right arm lifting overhead, then straightens. From 9.0 seconds to the end she leans close into the lens, points a playful finger-gun straight at the camera and grins widely, settling into that pointing smile as the final pose. Her lips move in sync with the music throughout the clip.

overall_soundscape: Warm indoor room tone with faint HVAC hum. The girl's footsteps make soft padded taps on the carpet as she bounces; her braids and clothing produce no distinct sound. Her voice is not clearly audible as words, only musical mouth movement synced to the track.

non_diegetic_music: An upbeat, cute Chinese dance-pop track at approximately 128 BPM with a steady kick drum on every beat, bright synth stabs on the strong accents, sparse in the first three seconds and fully grooving from the 5-second mark onward.
```

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action, vertical 9:16, a static full-body shot frames a young Chinese girl with low twin pigtails and straight bangs standing centered in a bright modern apartment living room. She wears an oversized cream sweatshirt printed with a large red-gingham bow and small red text, a matching cream pleated drop-waist skirt, cream slouchy leg warmers and black buckle mary-jane shoes. Behind her are floor-to-ceiling windows with a daytime city skyline, a white tower fan, a sheer white curtain, and a white console along the left wall lined with a neat row of plush toys. The camera stays completely still. From 0.0 to 0.5 seconds she pops a pose with her right hand on her hip and her head tilted, then at 0.5 seconds lifts that hand to her chin in a shy gesture. From 0.8 to 1.3 seconds she grabs her left pigtail and tosses it back over her shoulder, the hair sweeping in a soft arc. From 1.3 to 2.0 seconds she turns her body left and back to center with a small step, arms swinging outward and down. From 2.0 to 5.0 seconds she settles with both hands clasped low in front of her waist, gently swaying her hips and tilting her head in a slow bounce on the beat. At 5.0 seconds she releases her hands to her sides, and from 5.8 to 6.8 seconds raises both arms to hip height, pumps her right fist upward once, and does a small bouncy step-open-and-close. From 6.8 seconds to the end she stands facing the camera with both arms spread slightly out to her sides and holds that stance.

overall_soundscape: Quiet indoor room tone with a faint HVAC hum from the tower fan. Soft carpeted footsteps and the light swish of her sleeve fabric during the arm moves; the plush toys and window glass stay silent.

non_diegetic_music: A gentle, cute Chinese pop track at approximately 64 BPM with a soft kick on each slow beat, light hand-percussion subdivisions between beats, and a sweet toy-like synth melody.
```

## ② 场景与人物档案

**场景**（observed）：白天玻璃幕墙咖啡厅/食堂内。浅木色方桌，桌上白圆盘盛蛋包饭（黄色蛋皮+红色番茄酱竖条纹），盘左侧桌面放一部手机；背景为大幅玻璃窗，窗外绿树、遮阳伞与行人虚化；画面右后有吊灯、木椅；地面浅色瓷砖。自然采光。

**人物 1（主舞台）**（observed）：猫耳女仆装扮少女，约 18-22 岁观感。
- 头部：银白色长假发（侧编麻花辫垂至胸前+两侧发髻），头顶大型蓬松粉白猫耳（粉色内耳），双耳基部各系黑色蝴蝶结（带银色小铃/爱心坠）。
- 妆容：超现实"瓷娃娃"妆——零毛孔均净肤、灰绿虹膜、腮红重、粉唇；整体为重度滤镜/AI 感（见⑤）。
- 服装：白色蕾丝衬衫（领口黑色大蝴蝶结+心形水钻坠），黑色背带百褶短裙（肩带宽荷叶边），腰侧银色 D 环装饰，黑色连裤丝袜；**无鞋**（踩盘时为纯丝袜脚）。

**人物 2（POV 摄影者，仅局部入画）**（observed）：画框底部常驻其双手（交叠扶桌沿/扶膝），腕部黑色蕾丝手套/袖套（金线花卉刺绣），右腕珠链手链；10-15s 下摇时可见其自身腿部——黑色蕾丝丝袜（金色花卉刺绣纹样）并拢坐姿。面部不出画。

**画外行动者**：人物 2 即画外行动者（POV 主体），可见部分=双手+腿；控制物件=无（未触碰道具）。

**道具**：白圆盘+蛋包饭（被踩前后状态变化：条纹完整→压碎偏移）、手机、木桌椅、吊灯。

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：混合（AI 生成倾向偏高；未达决定性单判）**

- AI 倾向证据 1：人物 1 全距离零毛孔瓷面——1.8s/3.2s 近距面部特写仍无皮肤纹理、无汗毛，唇面呈釉质光泽；与已知"AI 猫耳女仆"风格签名一致。
- AI 倾向证据 2：5.30s 站→坐 0.1s 内跳变（无过渡帧），且切点前后构图/服装完全连续——AI 多段生成的跳变特征。
- 实拍倾向证据 1：POV 手部（11.5s 原分辨率）有真实指节褶皱、皮肤质感层次、珠链手链细节；蕾丝手套金线刺绣纹理连贯。
- 实拍倾向证据 2：食物被踩后的形变（条纹拖抹、蛋皮塌陷）跨帧保持物理一致（2s→5s→14s 压碎状态延续不重置）。
- 实拍倾向证据 3：背景玻璃窗外景自然景深与行人运动连续。
- 反证检查：人物面部跨 15s 同脸无漂移；猫耳头饰全程在位（站姿段与坐姿段均为同款大型粉白猫耳，早前"站姿无耳"判读经全分辨率复查收回）；无肢体融合、无穿模。
- 判定说明：AI 违例与实拍证据并存且均不决定性；按三档判"混合"。对提示词写作的影响：风格写"hyper-filtered doll-skin live-action"兼容两解。

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action dance clip with a heavy beautifying filter, vertical 9:16, a locked close shot frames a young Chinese woman from the chest up against a plain warm-beige wall with soft even lighting. She has shoulder-length dark hair with wispy bangs and wears a white square-neck crop top under a white shrug with long fitted sleeves, white full-finger gloves reaching mid-forearm, and black low-rise leggings with side drawstring cutouts. From 0.0 to 1.4 seconds she interlocks her thumbs in front of her chest with her remaining fingers pointing forward, rocking her head side to side while mouthing the English lyric, <d>[English] Get your hands up, everybody, body, body, body, body.</d> then draws both hands together at her chest. [Shot 2] At 00:01.450, the camera cuts to a locked medium shot framing her from the waist up in the same wall and lighting; her arms reset low as she swings her right arm down across her waist, crosses both arms low, then opens them outward while her hips start popping side to side. From 2.4 to 8.0 seconds she raises her left arm straight overhead with the elbow bent to frame her face, rotating her wrist in continuous circles and popping her index finger upward about once per second on the beat, while her hips keep riding the steady kick and her torso leans into each sway, her right elbow tucked at her waist. At 00:07.500 her right hand rises to meet the left so both fingertips frame her face.

overall_soundscape: Quiet indoor room tone with no reverb tail; the fabric of her shrug and gloves swishes softly on the arm swings and wrist rolls.

non_diegetic_music: An upbeat dance-pop track at about 128 BPM continues from the previous shot without restart — a sparse intro under the first line, then a steady four-on-the-floor kick grid from 4.4 seconds with an English vocal hook.
```

### SEG02｜源 00:08.00-00:15.56｜指天→比心→张手收尾

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action dance clip with a heavy beautifying filter, vertical 9:16, continuing the locked medium shot from the waist up against the plain warm-beige wall, same outfit and lighting, the music still riding its steady ~128 BPM kick. From 0.0 to 1.8 seconds she crosses both arms in front of her chest, flings her right hand down to her hip and swings her left arm outward. From 1.8 to 3.2 seconds she thrusts her right arm straight up with her index finger pointing at the ceiling, left hand on her hip, torso tilting left with her hip pushed right for two full beats. From 3.2 to 4.2 seconds she pulls both bent elbows in and points both index fingers toward the left side of frame with her head tilted left. From 4.2 to 5.2 seconds she closes both hands together in front of her chest into a heart shape, smiling with her teeth showing. From 5.2 to 5.9 seconds she spreads both hands beside her face with all fingers pointing up, framing her face. From 5.9 to 7.5 seconds she flings both arms wide past her shoulders with palms facing out, snaps them back in, and lets her hands fall to her sides with one last hip pop and a bright smile, still mid-groove as the clip cuts off without a hold.

overall_soundscape: The same quiet room tone continues; her gloves whisper on the wide arm throws and her heels stay silent as she dances in place.

non_diegetic_music: The same upbeat dance-pop track at about 128 BPM continues seamlessly without restart through its closing phrase, cut off exactly with the video's end.
```

## ④ BPM 实测（onset_probe.py）

- **BPM ≈ 65（中高置信）**：强音头 31.56/32.48/33.40/35.25/36.18/37.09/38.94/40.79/42.63/44.48/… 呈 **0.92s 等距**；自相关参考 64.6 一致；45-70s 段出现 0.44-0.46s 半拍细分（八分音符绸点）；74.43-77.95 为密集花击簇（drum fill/器乐华彩）。
- **0-17.7s 为自由板引子**：几乎无 onset（17.72 首音头），古风散板开场——该区间提示词不写 BPM 网格，按乐句气口锚定。
- 画面对账：31.56s 强 onset 与全曲第一个稳态舞段起点（31.8 全景亮相）吻合；77.95 花击簇对应 77.3-79.8 蹲起重拍段。

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：实拍（重滤镜/精修妆造），非 AI 生成。**
证据：①34.53s 原分辨率帧——真实皮肤毛孔、雀斑级肤色不均、发丝飞絮、美瞳反光自然，手指握扇骨关节正确；②48.03s 下腰帧——腰椎曲度、重心腿承重、裙布重力下垂方向全部物理正确，运动模糊方向与动作矢量一致；③52-53s 水袖翻转布料褶皱连续无熔断；④三场景道具（竹叶、屏风绢画、油纸伞伞骨）透视一致。重美颜痕迹：远距离舞蹈帧面部磨皮过度呈现「人偶感」、眼部精修放大——属滤镜层，不构成 AIGC 证据。未发现肢体穿模/背景漂移/细节闪烁等 AI 违例。

## ⑥ 剪辑特效层

- 硬切约 40 处（切点清单见时间轴「~」时刻，采样精度 ±0.4s），全部为 STAGE↔PORTRAIT↔LATTICE 场景/景别跳变，多数落在强 onset 上（卡点剪辑）。
- 0-2s→2.53s 疑似叠化（uncertain，密帧未决）。
- 暗房段白色花瓣飘落（95.3-98.5 明显）：成因 uncertain（实物抛撒或后期粒子），按可见形态「白色花瓣自上而下飘落」写入提示词。
- 无定格、无白闪、无字幕卡、无变速；全程平台水印「西海岸红杉 bilibili」（素材载体层）。

## ⑦ 完整提示词（T2VA 三核心字段 · 8 段，每段独立完整代码块）

> **使用方法**：每段配合**原片对应区间音频切片**使用（段元数据已标全局源区间）；段内时钟从 00:00 重算；音乐连续不重启。无参考图 → T2VA；如用户后续提供两套造型参考图，可升级 Ref2VA。

### SEG01｜源 0.00-13.80｜13.80s｜引子人像+首舞段（散板引子，禁 BPM）

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic guofeng music-video style, soft glow, slightly desaturated cool tones. A close portrait shot opens in a dark room of carved wooden cabinets and white blossom branches: a young woman in a water-blue hanfu gown with white feather shoulder pieces and blue flower hairpins slowly waves a white lace-ruffled fan beside her face, smiles and glances over her shoulder. [Shot 2] At 00:02.200, the camera cuts to a wide shot of a light-blue photo studio with a projected moon, a bamboo plant at left, a landscape folding screen at right and a red-bordered mat on the floor. The same performer, now in a coral-and-gold dance hanfu with peach double-bun hairstyle, crosses two long silk fans in pastel pink and blue above her head, then opens them into a wave as she steps. [Shot 3] At 00:04.800, the shot cuts back to a close portrait: she turns a small round silk fan beside her cheek. [Shot 4] At 00:06.300, the shot cuts to her seated pose holding a white lace parasol, petals drifting. [Shot 5] At 00:07.800, the shot cuts to a medium stage shot: she performs cloud-hand arm circles while sweeping the silk fans in flat circles at chest height. [Shot 6] At 00:11.300, the camera cuts to a wide shot as she travels across the mat with small gliding steps, fans trailing. The segment ends mid-phrase with her arms opening wide.

overall_soundscape: Quiet indoor room tone with a soft rain-like ambience in the background, silk fabric swishing gently on each fan movement and light footsteps on the mat.

non_diegetic_music: A slow Chinese-style guofeng ballad begins with a sparse free-tempo intro of soft plucked strings and airy pads, gradually introducing a gentle pulse without a firm dance beat yet.
```

### SEG02｜源 13.80-28.45｜14.65s｜旋转甩绸与进退步（稳态 ~65 BPM 起效）

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic guofeng style, light-blue studio stage. A close shot opens on the coral-hanfu dancer mid-spin, her teal silk fan sweeping horizontally across the frame with motion blur, hair buns whipping. [Shot 2] At 00:02.000, the camera cuts to a medium shot: she alternates the two silk fans in crossing arcs, stepping side to side on the beat. [Shot 3] At 00:04.000, the shot cuts closer as both arms spread wide and the silks unfurl to full length. [Shot 4] At 00:05.000, a medium shot follows her continuous phrase: fan circles flow into a low sweep, then rise. [Shot 5] At 00:06.500, a closer shot catches the fans crossing above her head in a quick double flourish. [Shot 6] At 00:07.500, the camera cuts to a wide shot: she advances and retreats along the mat, drawing figure-eight paths with both fans. [Shot 7] At 00:12.000, a medium-wide shot shows her wrapping the silks in one large circle around her body and sinking into a soft knee-bend, then rising. The segment ends as the silks settle behind her shoulders.

overall_soundscape: Silk fans swish and snap on each accent, soft footsteps cross the mat, and the studio stays otherwise quiet.

non_diegetic_music: A steady guofeng dance ballad at about 65 BPM with plucked-string pulses, soft woodblock accents and flowing string pads, entering its first stable groove.
```

```text
overall_soundscape: Fabric sweeps and a soft floor touch during the backbend, sleeve fabric billowing in the dark room, quiet room tone underneath.
```

# D026 反推档案

- **档案号**：D026（源 v11）

**场景**（observed）：夜间老街骑楼人行道。背景为民国风骑楼建筑：雕花石柱、镂空水泥花格窗、墙上中式字号牌匾（暖黄射灯打亮）、卷帘门商铺、壁挂空调外机与白色壁灯；地面灰色石板+白色划线；冷蓝夜空与暖黄街灯对比。氛围=城市夜拍街舞。

**人物（4 名，按 8.0s 画面从左到右登记）**（observed）：

| 编号 | 发型 | 上装 | 下装 | 鞋 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| P1（最左） | 黑短发 | 黑色印花短背心（露腰） | 黑色紧身短裤 | 黑色及膝袜+厚底靴 |
| P2（左二） | 黑长发 | 黑色透视印花长袖（链条配饰） | 铆钉装饰黑短裙 | 黑色高筒厚底靴 |
| P3（左三） | 金棕长发 | 灰黑拼色紧身上衣 | 铆钉超短裙 | 裸腿+黑色短靴 |
| P4（前右，最高） | 黑长直发 | 黑色透视紧身上衣（胸前露肤区） | 黑色流苏短裤 | 黑色过膝厚底靴 |

- 全员重度美颜（瓷面、放大眼、瘦腿），体型比例经滤镜极端化。
- 无人手持道具；画外行动者=拍摄者（未入画）。

| 0.00-1.20 | **队形揭示-1**：P4 站前位右手扶头侧定格摆 pose；P2 蹲伏隐于 P4 身后（画面内仅见其腿部与白色鞋尖） | 锁机广角，半身-全身 |
| 1.20-2.50 | **队形揭示-2**：P2 从 P4 左后方起立展开入画，两人开始同步胯部左右摆+手部小动作 | 同上 |

| 3.50-6.70 | 四人齐舞弱拍段：同步胯摆+双臂体侧波浪；5.0s 前后 P1 深蹲弹起（其余三人配合下蹲蓄力）；动作密度低（BGM 弱拍段） | 同上 |
| 6.70-9.50 | **鼓点 accent 段**（6.79s 起强 onset 网格）：四人同步手臂交叉胸前 hit→展开→重心左右切换；P4 前位领舞清晰 | 同上 |
| 9.50-11.50 | **横向行进**：全组保持队形整体向画面右侧移位两步，配合交叉臂 hit 与甩头（10.5s 前后 P4/P3 发甩动） | 同上 |
| 11.50-13.44 | **一字排开收尾**：四人横向排开同步点步+手臂开合；13.1-13.3s P4 侧踢腿展开、双臂平举，全组动作进行中被截断（**终态 ongoing**） | 同上 |

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：实拍（+重度美颜/瘦腿滤镜），中高置信**

- 证据 1（8.0s 全分辨率）：四人面部各自独立、表情与口型互不同步（自然群舞状态）；磨皮严重但五官结构、发际线、双眼皮形态真实且跨帧稳定。
- 证据 2：0-3.8s 起身入列编舞的遮挡关系物理一致——P2 蹲伏时仅腿部先于躯干可见、起身时裙摆与四肢交叠层级正确；此类复杂前后景遮挡为实拍强特征。
- 证据 3：石板地面+白色划线透视全程一致；街灯光源方向固定，人影/服装高光随动作连续变化。
- 证据 4：踢腿、甩发、转身动作带真实运动模糊（13.2s P4 侧踢模糊方向与脚速一致）。
- 反证检查：无肢体融合、无手指异常、无背景元素漂移；"超长腿/极细腰"为瘦腿滤镜效果，不构成 AI 证据。
- 保留项：夜间暗部噪点经降噪处理，极端情况下不排除局部 AI 增强；综合判实拍+滤镜。

## ⑦ 完整提示词（Ref2VA 六段，单段 13.44s，多人身份映射版）

> **需用户供图 1**：请用户提供一张含四名角色的参考图（或按 P1-P4 顺序可辨别的组合图）作为 `<Picture 1>` 锁定四人身份与服装；未供图时本提示词不可直接执行（T2VA 四人一致性无法保证）。`<Audio 1>` 为源片原声 BGM，随图一并供入；不供音频时删除 `<Audio 1>` 相关行并将两个声音字段改为 N/A。

```text
subject_definitions:
<Subject 1> is the far-LEFT dancer (P1) defined by <Picture 1>: short black hair, black printed cropped tank top, tight black shorts, black knee socks with chunky boots.
<Subject 2> is the second-from-LEFT dancer (P2) defined by <Picture 1>: long black hair, black sheer printed long-sleeve top with chain accessories, studded black mini skirt, black platform boots.
<Subject 3> is the third-from-LEFT dancer (P3) defined by <Picture 1>: long golden-brown hair, grey-and-black fitted top, studded micro skirt, bare legs with black ankle boots.
<Subject 4> is the front-RIGHT tallest dancer (P4) defined by <Picture 1>: long straight black hair, sheer black fitted top with an open chest panel, black fringed shorts, black over-knee platform boots.
<Video 1> is the source motion reference: its four performers define the entrance order, formation, choreography, timing and camera behavior. Its performers' real faces and filtered bodies are appearance references only and must be replaced by the four subjects from <Picture 1>.
<Audio 1> is the original BGM track of <Video 1>, reused as the complete audio of the target video.

summary:
[reference generation + audio reuse] A 13.4-second night-street girl-group dance in front of a lit Chinese arcade arcade building, featuring four performers <Subject 1>-<Subject 4> from <Picture 1>. <Picture 1> (governs the entire visual look) remains the authority for all four identities and outfits, while <Video 1> is referenced for the staggered-entrance formation, choreography, timing and locked wide framing only. The choreography combines hip-sway groove with arm-cross accents on a ~140 BPM beat, beginning from a solo front pose with a hidden crouching dancer and ending mid-motion in a spread line.

retention_analysis:
<Subject 1> (appears throughout [Shot 1] after 00:02.500): fully_preserved - identity, outfit and left position retained.
<Subject 2> (appears throughout [Shot 1]): fully_preserved - identity, outfit and second-left position retained.
<Subject 3> (appears throughout [Shot 1] after 00:02.500): fully_preserved - identity, outfit and third-left position retained.
<Subject 4> (appears throughout [Shot 1]): fully_preserved - identity, outfit and front-right lead position retained.
<Picture 1> (governs the entire visual look): fully_preserved - the four characters and their styling.
<Video 1> (governs the whole segment): reference - entrance order, formation, choreography, timing and camera framing are transferred; the original performers' faces and bodies are discarded.
<Audio 1>: fully_copy - <Audio 1> is reused 1:1 as the target video's complete final audio track.

detailed_description:
The target video is a night-street dance performance filmed in one locked wide shot on a stone-paved sidewalk in front of a lit Republican-era Chinese arcade building with carved columns, lattice concrete windows, warm-lit shop signboards and a cool blue dusk sky.
[Shot 1] From 00:00.000 to 00:01.200, <Subject 4> stands front-right with her right hand raised beside her head holding a poised opening pose while <Subject 2> stays crouched hidden behind her, only her lower legs and shoe tip visible below <Subject 4>'s skirt. From 00:01.200 to 00:02.500, <Subject 2> rises up into frame at <Subject 4>'s left and the two begin swaying their hips side to side with small hand motions in sync. From 00:02.500 to 00:03.500, <Subject 1> and <Subject 3> rise up one after another on the left, completing a staggered diagonal line of four. From 00:03.500 to 00:06.700, all four dance in loose sync through the quiet pre-drop section: hip sways with rippling arm waves at their sides, and around 00:05.000 <Subject 1> sinks into a low squat and springs back up while the others dip in support. From 00:06.700 to 00:09.500, on the drop of the beat, all four snap their arms crossed in front of their chests, open them wide, and switch weight left and right on each accent, <Subject 4> leading clearly at front-right. From 00:09.500 to 00:11.500, the group holds its formation and travels two steps toward the right of frame together with arm-cross hits and hair whips around 00:10.500 from <Subject 4> and <Subject 3>. From 00:11.500 to 00:13.440, the four spread into a level line, step in place and open and close their arms in unison; in the final moment <Subject 4> extends one leg into a side kick with both arms sweeping flat while the others hold their arm poses, the whole group still in motion as the clip cuts off without a hold.

OVERALL CONTINUITY: the same four performers keep their left-to-right identity positions throughout the entrances, travelling and line spread; no identity swaps, no added performers; the arcade building, lighting and pavement of the referenced night street remain unchanged; the camera stays locked as one wide shot.

overall_soundscape: The reused street-night ambience layer of <Audio 1> continues throughout — faint city murmur under the music, with the scuff of boots on stone pavement during the travelling steps.

non_diegetic_music: <Audio 1> is reused 1:1 as the complete final audio track, including its quiet pre-drop first six seconds, the ~139 BPM beat drop at 00:06.700 and the denser closing hits, ending exactly with the video cutoff.
```

# D027 档案《麦克风支架 Limbo 热舞》

**短标题↔原片名对照**：麦克风支架 Limbo 热舞 ↔ `至此 拐杖迎来了它最有用的一集.mp4`（批次源 v12；「拐杖」实为麦克风支架/话筒杆，被当 Limbo 横杆用）
**媒资建档**：720x1280 竖屏 / 28.75fps(VFR 感知) / h264 / 33.99s / 有音轨（现场 BGM+人群）/ 无平台水印可见
**观察门控**：COMPLETE（五遍读取+三窗密帧+末段倒查；现场音频仅 onset 实测，人声未核实）

---

## ① 片型分流

**A 型 · 一镜到底（continuous take，手持竖拍）**。
判据：33.99s 无任何切镜（三张 contact sheet 构图连续演变、人群站位不重置）；机位=拍摄者手持站定，中景略仰（镜头高约 1.4-1.5m，距主体约 4-5m），有轻微手持微晃与小幅度重新取景（跟随舞者重心），无推拉轨道运动。

## ② 场景与人物档案

**场景**（observed）：室内活动场地——淡紫灰色墙面、左侧两叠黑色线阵音箱、水泥地坪（微反光）、白色着装人群围成半圆围观（部分人举手机拍摄）；活动氛围（粉丝见面会/校园活动）。
**人物**：
- **舞者**（observed）：长波浪棕发（发量多），白色露腰衬衫（下摆系结、穿法滑肩）+白色短裤+黑色过膝高跟长靴；
- **举杆者×2**（observed）：左端白T恤黑裤男生、右端白T恤迷彩裤长发女生，全程将话筒支架横举过头（杆上带话筒夹），高度基本恒定；
- **围观人群**（observed）：20+ 人半圆站位，随节拍拍手、举手机。
**画外行动者**：拍摄者（手持镜头，第一人称视角在场）。
**后期叠加**（observed）：0.97-2.43s 左上角白色圆圈动画+文字（可辨「如约而至」与「limbo」字样，其余不可辨）；2.92-4.42s 文字「正式开始」。属后期字幕层。

## ④ BPM 实测（onset_probe.py）

- **BPM ≈ 126（中置信）**：自相关参考 126.0；强音头序列含 0.47-0.49s 与 0.72-0.73s 两级间隔（0.72≈1.5×0.48，附点/切分结构明显），非均匀网格——按「126 参考网格+强切分」交付，动作锚点以强 onset 为主（0.71/1.23/1.81/2.88/4.41/5.35/6.79/8.19/9.17/11.83/13.20/15.58/17.02/18.23/20.83/22.02/23.95/25.37/28.44/29.64/30.82/32.25）。

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：实拍（轻美颜），非 AI 生成。**
证据：①围观人群 20+ 人各有自然微动作（拍手节拍不同步、手机举放），AI 难以维持这种多人一致性；②水泥地坪反光与音箱透视逐帧稳定；③下腰时衬衫下摆重力下垂、长发后甩惯性方向正确（6.12s、22.85s 帧）；④手持微晃连续无「数字稳像」感；⑤高跟长靴与水泥地接触有合理形变阴影。肤色磨皮属常规美颜。

## ⑥ 剪辑特效层

无切镜、无变速、无转场。后期叠加层两处：①0.97-2.43s 白色圆圈描边动画+左上角文字（部分可辨）；②2.92-4.42s 文字「正式开始」。无定格/白闪/漫画线。音频层：现场 BGM+人群拍手欢呼（同期环境声，内容未逐字核实）。

## ⑦ 完整提示词（T2VA 三核心字段 · 3 段）

> 使用方法：每段配原片对应区间音频切片；段内时钟 00:00 重算；无参考图 → T2VA（可补图1锁舞者身份后升级 Ref2VA）。

### SEG01｜源 0.00-11.50｜11.50s｜过杆①②+热舞起势

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action handheld vertical video, casual event footage style, one unbroken take. In an indoor venue with pale lavender walls, black line-array speakers stacked at the left and a concrete floor, a crowd of about twenty onlookers in white outfits forms a half circle, many filming with phones. At center, a young woman with long wavy brown hair, wearing a knotted white cropped shirt worn off one shoulder, white shorts and black over-knee heeled boots, leans back deeply and passes under a horizontal microphone stand held overhead by a man in a white tee at the left end and a woman in a white tee and camouflage pants at the right end. She rises, grips the bar briefly with both hands and releases it. [Shot 2 continues unbroken] From 00:05.800 to 00:07.000 she arches back into a second, deeper limbo pass, spine curved, head tilted back, knees softly bent, hair hanging, then rises at 00:07.100 with a hair flip. From 00:08.300 to 00:11.500 she dances upright with hip sways and circling arm waves, the shirt hem swinging with each turn while the crowd claps along. The handheld camera only micro-shakes and reframes slightly; it never cuts.

overall_soundscape: Room echo with a mid-loud event crowd: continuous clapping on the beat, occasional cheers and phone-shutter clicks, and the soft thud of heeled boots on concrete.

non_diegetic_music: Upbeat dance-pop background music played at the venue, around 126 BPM with a strong syncopated groove, heard as the event's own playback.
```

### SEG02｜源 11.50-23.30｜11.80s｜最深过杆③+直立热舞+台步全转

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Same unbroken handheld take in the same venue. From 00:00.300 to 00:02.800 the dancer performs her deepest limbo pass: she arches backward under the horizontal microphone stand, back bowed fully, arms hanging loose, holds the under-bar position about two seconds while the crowd reacts, then rises slowly through 00:04.800. At 00:05.200 she throws both arms up in a small cheering hop. [Shot 2 continues unbroken] From 00:06.000 to 00:07.800 she dances upright, circling her arms and lifting one leg forward in a controlled high kick held for a beat at 00:07.300. From 00:07.900 to 00:09.800 she turns with continuous hip sways. From 00:09.900 to 00:11.800 she struts toward the camera with hands on hips, then spins a full turn in place at 00:11.200, her long hair whipping around as the segment ends mid-momentum.

overall_soundscape: The crowd claps and cheers louder around the deep limbo hold, heels tap the concrete, fabric swishes on the spin.

non_diegetic_music: The venue's dance-pop playback continues at about 126 BPM with syncopated accents matching the strut and spin.
```

### SEG03｜源 23.30-33.99｜10.69s｜过杆④-⑦+持杆收尾（进行中结束）

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Same unbroken handheld take. From 00:00.300 to 00:01.800 the dancer leans back through a fourth limbo pass under the microphone stand and rises. At 00:02.300 she does a quick shallow dip-and-rise pass. After straightening at 00:03.800, she performs another small backward pass around 00:04.300 to 00:04.800, rises with a hair flip at 00:05.300, then arches into a final deep pass from 00:06.200 to 00:07.700 with the bar passing behind her chest. [Shot 2 continues unbroken] From 00:08.200 to 00:09.400 she rises into her off-shoulder pose and struts with hip sways. From 00:09.600 to the end she reaches up, grips the horizontal bar with both hands and hangs from it briefly, then angles her body sideways against it holding the bar with one hand, legs crossed in a strut pose. The video ends while she is still moving in this pose, with no black frame, no caption and no settling.

overall_soundscape: Sustained crowd clapping and rising cheers around each pass, heels on concrete, fabric and hair swishes through the final pose.

non_diegetic_music: The venue's dance-pop playback keeps its about-126-BPM syncopated groove until the video cuts off mid-phrase.
```

- **短标题 ↔ 原片名**：粉系萌屋双娃应援舞 ↔ 萌妹子.mp4
- **时长**：13.70s（30fps，720×1280，411 帧）

- **观察状态**：COMPLETE（scope=visual+audio-dialogue+rhythm）
- **复述确认门**：批量流水线，waived（证据缺口照留）

|---|---|
| 画幅/时长 | 720×1280 竖屏 / 13.70s |

| 运镜 | 无推拉摇移 |
| 剪辑层 | 无切点、无定格、无变速、无字幕贴纸水印（早前缩略图"粉色水印"经原分辨率复查为背景窗帘/玩偶，判读收回） |
| **ABC 结论** | **A 一镜到底**（锁机基线） |

## ② 场景与人物档案

**场景**（observed）：粉色系儿童房/萌系直播间一角。背景：白墙+粉色心形花纹窗帘（右侧）、粉色置物架（摆粉色双肩包、独角兽毛绒玩偶、红色座椅）、地面拼色泡沫爬行垫（黄/蓝/粉拼块+粉白图案地垫）。室内柔光。

**人物 1（左）**（observed）：学龄女童（约 6-10 岁观感）。
- 发型：黑色齐刘海+双马尾（粉色波点蝴蝶结发绳）。
- 服装：粉色开衫外套，内搭粉白印花 T 恤（卡通图案），深藏青百褶短裙，粉白条纹过膝袜；**颈部戴粉色花朵形卡通颈枕（My Melody 风）**。
- 0-1s 手持一只粉色毛绒玩偶。

**人物 2（右）**（observed）：学龄女童（与人物 1 同龄观感，似姐妹/双胞胎装扮）。
- 发型：黑色齐刘海+双马尾（粉色波点蝴蝶结）。
- 服装：浅蓝色开衫外套，同款粉白印花 T 恤，深藏青百褶短裙，粉白条纹过膝袜；**颈部戴蓝白色云朵形卡通颈枕（玉桂狗风）**。

**画外行动者**：无（拍摄者未入画）。

**道具**：粉色毛绒玩偶（人物 1 手持，1s 后离手出画下缘）；两只卡通颈枕（穿戴）；背景玩偶/书包（静态）。

## ③ 全程时间轴

| 时间码 | 主体动作（因果链） | 机位/构图 |
|---|---|---|
| 0.00-1.00 | 人物 1 双手持粉色毛绒玩偶于胸前，人物 2 抬右手整理马尾/发绳；两人站定面向镜头 | 锁机全身微俯 |
| 1.00-2.50 | 玩偶下移出画；两人并拢站位，双手开始抬起预备；念唱 BGM 已开始（0.67s 起） | 同上 |

| 6.50-8.60 | **拳托腮萌姿段**：双手握拳叠于下巴下方做"萌姿"，胯部左右轻顶，两人笑容（6.7s 人物 1 闭嘴笑、人物 2 低头笑） | 同上 |

**末三分之一倒查**：最后清楚可见状态=两人站姿摆动中、人物 2 右手抬起；其后无黑场/字卡/转场；动作仍在进行中被文件终点截断。终帧 13.66s 已核。

## ④ BPM 实测（onset_probe.py）

- ref_tempo = 62.3 BPM（autocorr，为半速候选）
- 强/H onset：4.41H 4.81H 5.76H 6.13H 6.25S 6.72S 7.20S 7.69S 8.15S 8.64H 9.11S 9.60H 10.07S 10.57H 11.03S 11.53S 11.99S 12.96H 13.44H

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：实拍（+重度美颜滤镜），高置信**

- 证据 1（6.7s 原分辨率面部特写）：两女童面部为**真实儿童结构**——圆润脸颊、短鼻梁、大额头、儿童比例五官；重滤镜仅磨皮放大眼部，骨相无法伪造。
- 证据 2：两人动作存在自然异步（人物 2 滞后 0.1-0.2s），同步率非完美——真实儿童跟跳特征（AI 群舞常过于整齐或错乱无规律）。
- 证据 3：背景物品（粉色书包、独角兽玩偶、心形窗帘、泡沫垫拼缝）透视一致且全程静止，无重绘漂移。
- 证据 4：挥拳/捂脸动作带真实运动模糊，颈枕随身体晃动有真实形变滞后。
- 反证检查：无肢体融合、无手指异常（2.5-4.5s 挥拳段指节正常）；无背景闪烁。

Behind them stand a pink shelf with a pink backpack and a plush unicorn, pink heart-patterned curtains, and a pastel foam play mat on the floor. # D031 档案《黑帽长裙帅气爵士舞》

**短标题↔原片名对照**：黑帽长裙帅气爵士舞 ↔ `帅气喵.mp4`（批次源 v16）
**媒资建档**：720x1280 竖屏 / 30fps / h264 / 25.17s / 有音轨 / 左上角「小雨霖ovo bilibili」水印
**观察门控**：COMPLETE（全片 50 帧覆盖 0-24.97s + 片尾密帧倒查；onset 实测）

---

## ① 片型分流

**A 型 · 一镜到底（continuous take，锁定机位）**。
判据：25s 单一构图（墙面暖光灯锥、深木墙裙、地面全程不变），舞者连续舞动无台阶式静止；机位=固定平视全身景（镜头高约 1.2-1.3m，距主体约 3.5m，正对），零推拉摇移。22.93-23.40s 人物出现连续强运动模糊而背景锐利——**可见形态**：人物快速运动+可能后期动感模糊强化（成因 uncertain），不构成切镜。

## ② 场景与人物档案

**场景**（observed）：室内墙角——米灰色壁纸墙面、深棕色木质墙裙踢脚线、墙面一盏暖光壁灯在头顶形成光锥、浅色石纹地面；无其他道具。
**人物**（observed）：单人女舞者——黑色宽檐礼帽（帽侧黑色羽饰）、黑色立领短款长袖上衣（露腰）、黑色高腰大摆长裙（及踝，裙摆极大）、黑色玛丽珍高跟凉鞋；长发披肩，妆容精致（红唇），美颜滤镜重（肤质瓷感）。
**画外行动者**：无。

## ③ 全程时间轴（0.000-25.167，含末段倒查）

| 时间 | 事件 | 事实态 |
|---|---|---|
| 0.00-1.00 | 站姿亮相：右手扶帽檐，身体微侧 | observed |
| 1.00-2.50 | 手指沿帽檐下滑离开，手腕翻转划弧 | observed |
| 2.50-4.50 | 双手举至帽侧再向外推开，腕部甩点（爪式手型） | observed |
| 4.50-6.00 | 手臂下扫+顶胯换边 | observed |
| 6.00-8.00 | 侧步移动，大裙摆左右甩荡（7.03-7.53 裙摆峰值） | observed |
| 8.00-9.50 | 肩部律动+手臂交叉胸前沿 | observed |
| 9.50-11.50 | 双手扶腰际+躯干波浪，重心左右 | observed |
| 11.50-13.50 | 点步+单手指向上方指空（12.53） | observed |
| 13.50-15.50 | 大幅度左右走位，裙摆大幅翻飞（14.03 峰值） | observed |
| 15.50-17.50 | 手臂波浪传递+手抚领口 | observed |
| 17.50-19.50 | 触帽+腕部绕环 | observed |
| 19.50-21.50 | 手臂rolling+侧伸腿点地（21.03 手臂外甩） | observed |
| 21.50-22.90 | 收步回中，双手腹前交叉 | observed |
| 22.90-23.40 | 快速变姿势——人物全程强运动模糊（背景锐利；是否后期模糊 uncertain） | observed(模糊成因 uncertain) |
| 23.50-25.17 | **终态定格**：右手上举至脸侧（软腕猫爪手）、左手垂于裙前、左腿向侧伸直绷脚尖点地，头部微偏——保持至最后可解码帧 24.97，settled | observed（末段倒查：无黑场无转场） |

三条变化轨：主体动作如上；镜头运动=全程锁定零运动（observed）；环境图形=仅平台水印恒定。

## ④ BPM 实测（onset_probe.py）

- **主强拍 ≈ 54 BPM（等距高置信；等效半拍网格 108 BPM 可选）**：强音头 7.02/8.14/9.25/10.37/11.48/12.59/13.70/14.81/15.93/17.04/18.15/19.26/20.36/21.49 呈 **1.11-1.12s 等距**；H 级细分 5.09/5.41/5.92、18.41/18.72/18.77 落于半拍（0.555s），构成 108 半拍细分网格。
- 自相关参考 107.7（=2×54）与网格相容。
- 画面对账：裙摆峰值 14.03≈14.81 onset 前、点步指空 12.53≈12.59 onset、终态定格≈23.15-23.71 onset 簇后收束。

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：实拍（重美颜滤镜），非 AI 生成。**
证据：①大裙摆物理真实——13.50-15.50 走位时裙布惯性滞后与褶皱走向连续（14.03 帧）；②22.93-23.40 快速动作的运动模糊方向与肢体矢量一致（AI 模糊通常整帧均匀）；③帽上羽饰随头部动作轻颤（18.03 帧）；④高跟凉鞋与地面接触阴影稳定；⑤手指关节（猫爪手 24.47 帧）数量形态正常。瓷感肤色+眼部放大属美颜滤镜层。未发现肢体穿模/边缘熔化等 AI 违例。

## ⑥ 剪辑特效层

无切镜、无定格、无转场。22.93-23.40s 人物运动模糊异常增强（成因 uncertain：现场快速动作+慢门/或后期动感模糊），不改变镜头连续性。全程平台水印「小雨霖ovo bilibili」。

### SEG01｜源 0.00-13.00｜13.00s｜亮相与上身律动段

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action vertical dance cover, one unbroken locked shot. A solo female dancer stands full-body in an indoor corner with beige wallpaper, dark wooden wainscoting and a warm wall lamp casting a cone of light above her. She wears a black wide-brim fedora with a black feather, a black high-neck cropped long-sleeve top with a bare midriff, a huge flowing black maxi skirt and black Mary-Jane heels. From 00:00.000 to 00:02.500 she holds a cool sideways stance with her right hand on the hat brim, slides her fingers off the brim and rolls her wrist in a slow arc. From 00:02.500 to 00:04.500 she raises both hands beside the hat then flicks them outward with cat-paw wrists. From 00:04.500 to 00:06.000 she sweeps one arm down while popping her hips to the other side. From 00:06.000 to 00:08.000 she side-steps so the giant skirt swings wide left and right. From 00:08.000 to 00:09.500 she pops her shoulders with her arms crossed low in front. From 00:09.500 to 00:11.500 she rests both hands at her waist and rolls her torso in a smooth wave, weight shifting side to side. From 00:11.500 to 00:13.000 she step-touches and points one finger straight up. The camera stays absolutely still throughout; no cuts, no zoom, no drift.

overall_soundscape: Quiet indoor room tone with a faint lamp hum; crisp heel clicks and the heavy swish of the long skirt on each side-step.

non_diegetic_music: A smooth mid-tempo pop-funk groove with steady strong accents about 1.1 seconds apart (about 54 BPM pulse with a 108 BPM half-beat feel), soft kick, finger snaps and a rounded bass line.
```

### SEG02｜源 13.00-25.17｜12.17s｜大走位段+终态定格

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Same unbroken locked shot. From 00:00.000 to 00:02.500 the dancer travels left and right across the corner in big strides, the black skirt billowing dramatically with each change of direction. From 00:02.500 to 00:04.500 she rolls her arms in sequential waves and draws one hand across her collarbone. From 00:04.500 to 00:06.500 she touches the hat brim again and circles her wrists. From 00:06.500 to 00:08.500 she rolls both shoulders and arms and flicks one arm outward while extending a leg to tap the floor. From 00:08.500 to 00:09.900 she gathers her steps back to center with her hands crossing in front of her waist. Around 00:09.900 to 00:10.400 she snaps into the final pose through a burst of strong motion blur on her figure while the background stays sharp. From 00:10.500 to the end she holds the finishing pose almost perfectly still: right hand raised beside her face with a soft cat-paw wrist, left hand resting on the skirt in front, left leg extended straight to the side with pointed toe, head tilted slightly. The pose is settled through the final frame; no black frame, no caption, no further movement.

overall_soundscape: Skirt fabric billowing on the travels, a single crisp heel tap on the leg extension, then near-silence as she holds the final pose.

non_diegetic_music: The same steady pop-funk groove continues with accents about 1.1 seconds apart, thinning slightly as the final pose lands on a strong accent.
```

# D033 档案《银杏道「逮捕」手势舞》

**媒资建档**：720x1280 竖屏 / 30fps / h264 / 32.13s（有效画面约至 30.2s，其后黑场尾段）/ 有音轨 / 右上角「531号芋圆 bilibili」水印+前段左上角红色圆形贴纸（素材载体层）

## ① 片型分流

**A 型 · 一镜到底（continuous take，锁定机位）**。
判据：有效画面段 0-30.2s 构图完全不变（银杏道、灯柱、地砖缝逐帧对齐），舞者连续舞动；机位=固定平视全身竖拍（镜头高约 1.2m，距主体约 4m，正对），零运镜。30.0-30.3s 转黑场（淡出/硬切边界 uncertain：overview 网格 30.53 处见暗化残影、密帧 30.33 已全黑），30.3-32.13s 为带水印黑场。

## ② 场景与人物档案

**场景**（observed）：秋日户外公园石板步道——两侧金色银杏树冠（顶部落满黄叶）、步道纵深、远处白色栏杆与树林、红色灯笼挂于枝间、阳光强烈，地面落大量树叶阴影（光斑锐利）。

**画外行动者**：无。

### SEG01｜源 0.00-11.00｜11.00s｜开场拳点段

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action vertical dance cover, one unbroken locked frontal full-body shot. A solo young woman dances on a stone park path in autumn: golden ginkgo canopies overhead, red lanterns hanging between branches, strong sunlight casting sharp leaf shadows on the pavement. She wears a navy JK-style cropped cardigan and pleated mini skirt with a pink bunny graphic, white thigh-high socks and black Mary-Jane shoes, with long brown hair and a white bow headband. From 00:00.000 to 00:01.000 she stands with hands resting at her skirt. From 00:01.000 to 00:02.000 she brings both hands to her chest with a small hop opening her stance. From 00:02.000 to 00:03.500 she punches one fist up while lifting the opposite knee. From 00:03.500 to 00:04.500 she plants both hands on her hips and pops them sideways. From 00:04.500 to 00:06.000 she alternates pointing gestures toward the camera, weight rocking side to side. From 00:06.000 to 00:08.000 she swings both arms widely while side-stepping. From 00:08.000 to 00:10.000 she rolls her arms continuously with springy knee bends. From 00:10.000 to 00:11.000 she jabs both fists forward twice with a small hop. The camera never moves; no cuts.

overall_soundscape: Bright outdoor park ambience with light wind in the ginkgo leaves, crisp heel taps on the stone pavement and skirt fabric swishing on the side-steps.

non_diegetic_music: An upbeat bright dance-pop track at about 147 BPM with a driving four-on-the-floor kick, claps on the backbeat and a catchy synth hook.
```

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：混合——实拍人物主体（高置信）+ 数字合成背景与前景层。**
实拍证据（0.47s 原分辨率帧）：真实皮肤质感与毛孔、假发纤维分离清晰、手指握扇骨关节正确、白色毛绒肩饰蓬松立体、金色挂件细节一致、跳跃腾空时裙布与飘带惯性方向正确（12.60 帧）；运动模糊帧（6.80）为真实快门模糊。
合成证据：①前景巨扇全程零位移零视差（15.80-16.63 人物从其前方经过无遮挡关系变化异常）；②背景渐变蓝无云层变化、花枝静止无摆动；③地面倒影在人物腾空时仍贴脚位置（12.60 倒影未随腾空分离/缩小，物理不严格）；④整体色调高度统一为后期调色。未见肢体穿模等整帧 AI 生成违例，故不判「AI 高置信」。

## ⑥ 剪辑特效层

无切镜、无定格、无字幕卡。后期合成层：蓝渐变背景、桃花枝、前景两把巨型静止折扇、地面倒影、整体调色。6.80s 运动模糊为拍摄/后期强化（成因 uncertain）。水印「晓丹小仙女儿 bilibili」。

### SEG01｜源 0.00-8.50｜8.50s｜回眸开场+双扇展示+旋身

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action cosplay dance video with a digitally composited set, one unbroken locked frontal full-body shot. The set is a clean light-blue gradient sky backdrop with a pink blossom branch entering from the upper right, a glossy light-blue reflective floor, and two giant pink folding fans composited as static foreground layers at the lower left and mid left. A young woman cosplays a fox spirit: long pink-to-purple wavy hair, white fox-ear headpiece, a white-and-red hanfu-style short dress with fluffy white shoulder trim, red sash and flowing ribbons, white legwear and black heels, a red bell choker and a small gold gourd at her waist. She starts turned half-away, both closed fans held up covering her face, then turns to the camera with a smile raising the right fan overhead by 00:01.900. From 00:01.900 to 00:03.400 she snaps the right fan fully open high and displays it, both fans opening horizontally wide at 00:02.900. From 00:03.400 to 00:05.300 she folds the fans and does small bouncy knee-lift hops, hides her face behind one fan and sweeps it aside. From 00:05.300 to 00:06.300 both fans wave in mirrored arcs and one fan covers the lower half of her face. At 00:06.800 she spins quickly in place with strong motion blur, then from 00:07.300 to 00:08.500 she comes out of the spin presenting both fans in a flat T with one fan resting on her shoulder. The camera never moves; the foreground fan layers stay perfectly static; no cuts.

overall_soundscape: A clean studio-quiet bed with crisp fan snaps on each opening, light heel taps on the glossy floor and soft ribbon swishes.

non_diegetic_music: An upbeat Chinese-style pop track at about 69 BPM with heavy syncopation, plucked-string riffs in sixteenth-note runs and bright percussion accents.
```

### SEG02｜源 8.50-16.87｜8.37s｜大扇遮身移步+终态定格

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Same unbroken locked shot with the same composited set. From 00:00.000 to 00:01.200 the dancer step-touches while opening and closing both fans in two beats. From 00:01.700 to 00:02.700 she hops lifting one knee and sweeps a fan across her front. From 00:03.100 to 00:04.600 she raises one giant fan shielding the left half of her body and sidesteps to the right to reveal herself again, the fan momentarily showing its plain back. From 00:05.100 to 00:06.100 she opens both fans wide in a symmetrical turn. From 00:06.500 to 00:07.100 she lifts a single fan overhead covering her face. From 00:07.100 to 00:07.600 she lowers the fan diagonally in front of her body, leaning back with one leg extended and pointed. From 00:07.600 to the end she holds this final pose nearly still: both hands gripping the giant pink fan diagonally across her, fox ears upright, smiling at the camera, settled through the final frame with no black frame and no further movement. The foreground fan layers remain perfectly static throughout; no cuts.

overall_soundscape: Final fan snap and a soft heel slide on the glossy floor, then near-silence as she holds the ending pose.

non_diegetic_music: The syncopated 69 BPM Chinese-style pop groove lands its last accent as the fan lowers, thinning into the held final pose.
```

```text
<Subject 1> is the LEAD dancer defined by <Picture 1>: long wavy dark-brown hair with red-tinted ends, a fitted grey-black double-breasted blazer dress with white collar and white cuff turn-ups, a white ID badge on a lanyard, bare legs and black patent stiletto heels.

The segment begins from an open hip-circle groove and ends in a crossed-arms reset.

<Subject 2> (appears at the right edge near 00:05.000): partially_preserved - only a leg and partial body enter the frame edge.

detailed_description:
The target video is a vertical handheld fancam of a mall atrium stage, LED screen glowing blue-purple behind, black speaker stacks and flight cases at the sides, patterned stage vinyl underfoot.
[Shot 1] From 00:00.000 to 00:04.500, <Subject 1> stands with her right hand on her hip and left hand low, weight on her right leg; she rolls her hips in slow circles while her left arm waves upward, then steps left as both arms swing down. From 00:04.500 to 00:09.500 she gathers both fists under her chin and bounces on the beat, lifting her knees for two marching steps forward-left, then opens into a wide stance bouncing with both arms up in a V. From 00:09.500 to 00:14.000 she does a step-touch to the right while pointing her left hand to the left, rolls her torso with a hair flick, and crosses both arms over her chest. From 00:14.000 to 00:15.000 she holds the crossed-arms reset as the segment hands off.

overall_soundscape: Mall atrium room tone under the music; her heels click on the stage vinyl during the marching steps and the badge lanyard taps her chest on the bounces.
```

# D039 档案《云裳谣·仙气水袖舞》

**短标题↔原片名对照**：云裳谣·仙气水袖舞 ↔ `在远方看谁人眉黛含笑~『云裳谣』.mp4`（批次源 v24）
**媒资建档**：1280x640 横屏 / 25fps / h264 / 92.80s / 有音轨（古风流行 BGM）/ 左上角「w千岛酱w bilibili」水印
**观察门控**：COMPLETE（全片 186 帧覆盖 0-92.56s + 原分辨率 AIGC 取证 + 片尾密帧倒查；onset 实测）

---

## ① 片型分流

**C 型 · 多镜头快剪（multi-shot montage）**。
判据：同一棚景内约 30 个硬切，全景舞段与特写插入（腿部/面部/水袖）交替，切点挂乐句；无跨镜连续相机旅程。**命名机位语法**：
- `WIDE`：棚景全景（舞者全身+月亮+花窗框）；
- `PORTRAIT`：上半身特写（面部/肩部）；
- `INSERT-FEET`：腿部/脚踝丝带特写；
- `CLOSE-SLEEVE`：水袖翻飞近景。
每拍用 assigned setup，切镜挂 onset；不套 LOCK/BURST。

### SEG01｜源 0.00-14.70｜14.70s｜特写开场+全景行进

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Dreamy pastel guofeng music-video style with soft blown-out highlights and milky tones, multi-shot montage. The set is a physical studio: pink-blue gradient cloth backdrop, a round moon prop, wooden Chinese lattice frames, glowing round lanterns, cloud-like cotton props and blue-white flower arrangements. [Shot 1 opens] An extreme close-up shows the dancer's crossed legs and ankles tied with light-blue ribbons as she sits among the clouds. [Shot 2] At 00:02.000, a wide shot: she rises with her back to the camera and swings her long ribbon water-sleeves. [Shot 3] At 00:03.000, a portrait close-up: she smiles and draws a hand across her chest, double hair buns and long twin strands with blue ornaments. [Shot 4] At 00:04.900, back to the wide shot: she travels through the set with gliding steps, sweeping the extra-long sleeves in flat arcs past the moon prop. [Shot 5] At 00:10.400, an insert returns to the crossed-leg close-up, then a cheek-resting portrait at 00:11.400. [Shot 6] At 00:11.900, the wide shot resumes her traveling phrase with sleeve sweeps. The cuts land on musical accents; each setup stays locked internally with no camera travel.

overall_soundscape: Soft dreamy room tone with fabric ribbons swishing, gentle heel steps over the cotton clouds, and a faint glowing-lantern hum.

non_diegetic_music: A Chinese-style pop ballad at about 138 BPM with a sparse free-tempo intro of plucked strings, percussion entering softly around the eighth second.
```

[Shot 7] At 00:13.000, a final wide phrase ends the segment mid-step. All cuts land on accents; setups stay locked.

From 00:07.000 to 00:08.200 she props her chin on one hand and raises her eyebrows with her mouth slightly open. ## ② 场景与人物档案

**场景**（observed）：室内/半室内工业风楼梯间。白色金属网格围栏（方格网片）+白色圆管扶手（斜向），灰色水泥台阶，围栏后为暗色空间（玻璃反光、堆放物），**网格后靠一只牛皮纸箱**；光线为顶部冷白荧光感照明，人物面部补光充足。

**人物**（observed）：年轻女性一人，约 20-25 岁观感。
- 发型：深棕色超长卷发（波浪大卷垂至腰下），中分无刘海。
- 妆容：极端滤镜妆——瓷白零毛孔、灰黑大虹膜、粉唇；整体"瓷娃娃"感（见⑤）。
- 服装：黑色紧身吊带包臀迷你裙；**蓝色过膝袜**（膝上起点，光照下小腿段呈浅蓝高光）；黑色尖头细跟高跟鞋。

**画外行动者**：无。

**道具**：无手持物。

## ③ 全程时间轴

| 时间码 | 主体动作（因果链） | 机位/构图 |
|---|---|---|
| 0.00-0.70 | **深蹲定格位**：蹲于台阶上，左腿沿台阶边缘下垂伸直（脚尖绷直点地），右手撩起右侧发丝，面向镜头 | 手持近景，人物居中偏右 |
| 0.70-1.50 | 右手收回抚至锁骨/胸口，躯干微左转，目光保持镜头 | 同上 |
| 1.50-2.50 | 右手向上撩起发尾（发丝扬起后落下），头随手微低再抬 | 同上 |
| 2.50-3.50 | 双手落至大腿/膝上，蹲姿重心微调（膝部并拢又分开小幅变化） | 同上 |
| 3.50-4.50 | 右手抬至耳侧抚脸/别发，头右倾、下巴收 | 同上 |
| 4.50-5.70 | 再次撩发后手落于肩前，眼睑垂落看向镜头下方——**姿态进行中被截断（终态 ongoing）** | 同上 |

**末三分之一倒查**：最后清楚可见状态=手落肩前、目光下垂的蹲姿；其后无黑场/字卡；动作仍在微调中被文件终点截断。终帧 5.66s 已核。

## ⑤ AIGC 判定（帧证据）

**结论：实拍（+极端美颜滤镜），中置信**

- 实拍证据 1：背景深度链真实——网格围栏方格透视随镜头微移连续变化，纸箱、扶手、玻璃反光层次一致；无重绘漂移。
- 实拍证据 2：发丝物理——1.5-2.5s 撩发动作中发丝成缕扬起并以惯性落下（真实发群动力学）。
- 实拍证据 3：蹲姿重心合理——脚掌贴台阶面、膝部开合与重心微移对应。
- 实拍证据 4：袜面高光随小腿角度连续变化（0.0s 与 2.5s 光带位置一致于光源方向）。
- AI 未证实项：皮肤全距离零毛孔、虹膜超大、唇面釉质化——极端滤镜可解释；4 项针对性检验（背景像素/发丝/重心/袜面）均未发现决定性 AI 违例（与 v05/v19 同族判定逻辑）。
- 判定说明：按"实拍+极端滤镜"交付；不排除 AI 增强管线，缺口表注明。

```text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action glamour portrait with an extreme beautifying filter, vertical 9:16, a handheld medium shot frames a young woman crouching on grey concrete steps inside an industrial stairwell with white wire-mesh fence panels, a white tubular handrail crossing diagonally behind her, and a cardboard box visible beyond the mesh under cool fluorescent-style lighting. She has very long dark-brown wavy hair falling past her waist and wears a black strappy bodycon mini dress, blue over-knee stockings and black pointed stiletto heels. From 0.0 to 0.7 seconds she holds a deep crouch on the steps with her left leg extended down along the step edge, toes pointed to the ground, while her right hand lifts a lock of hair on her right side and she gazes at the lens. From 0.7 to 1.5 seconds she draws that hand in to rest on her collarbone as her torso turns slightly left. From 1.5 to 2.5 seconds she lifts the same hand to toss the ends of her hair upward, the strands rising and falling with their own weight as her head dips and lifts with the motion. From 2.5 to 3.5 seconds both hands settle onto her thigh near the knee and her crouch shifts with small weight adjustments, knees closing and opening slightly. From 3.5 to 4.5 seconds she raises her right hand to her ear, tucking hair back with her head tilted right and chin lowered. From 4.5 to 5.7 seconds she sweeps the hair once more and lets her hand fall in front of her shoulder, her eyelids lowering as she keeps looking toward the lens, mid-pose as the clip cuts off without a hold.

overall_soundscape: Quiet stairwell room tone with a faint fluorescent hum; her stockings whisper on the concrete and her hair swishes softly on each toss.

non_diegetic_music: A soft rhythmic backing track continues underneath without a reliable beat grid to state, staying low and steady until the video cuts off.
```
